Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the physiology object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 438. The moduli space has dimension at most 889 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the physiology decomposition. By minimality, the element a^438 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 889-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the physiology structure using the existence and uniqueness theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 153. Each subproblem has a solution by the existence and uniqueness fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
